- [Learning: `gh pr update-branch` silently drifts both lockfiles; the `lockfile-sync` gate pins npm@11](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-06-30-update-branch-drifts-lockfiles-and-npm11-pin.md)
- [A bug that recurs across multiple merged fixes is a mis-diagnosis, not an incomplete fix — make the silent failure loud before fixing again](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-01-recurring-bug-across-merged-fixes-means-misdiagnosis-make-the-silent-failure-loud-first.md)
- [Learning: Concurrent Claude sessions on one worktree silently destroy uncommitted work](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-02-concurrent-session-collision-on-shared-worktree.md)
- [A merged fix is not a deployed fix on Concierge; instrument the blind surface, never ask the operator to run diagnostics](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-02-merged-is-not-deployed-on-concierge-instrument-dont-ask.md)
- [Clearing a masking CI wedge exposes a chain of latent defects — verify each fix by running the pipeline, file each as its own issue, and know when the wall is host-visibility](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-03-chain-of-latent-defects-clearing-a-wedge-exposes-a-cascade.md)
- [Applying an unmerged migration to shared dev during /work broke another PR's CI (and triggered a same-subsystem sibling collision at merge)](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-03-dev-apply-during-work-broke-shared-tenant-integration-and-sibling-collision.md)
- [Human-in-the-loop infra steps are an automation bug, not a runbook step](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-04-human-in-the-loop-infra-steps-are-an-automation-bug-not-a-runbook-step.md)
- [Learning: A plan that persists a record into the PR body must target `ship` (sole body author, full-replace) — and a non-technical operator only sees `action-required` issues, not PR bodies](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-04-plan-persisting-a-record-into-the-pr-body-must-target-ship-not-work.md)
- [ADR ordinal collision on rebase: renumber YOUR ADRs, protect main's — key on the discriminating issue number](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-05-adr-ordinal-collision-on-rebase-renumber-mine-not-mains.md)
- [Learning: a mid-pipeline ADR renumber must sweep the plan/tasks/AC — not just the ADR body](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-05-adr-renumber-must-sweep-planning-docs-and-scripts-glob-orphan.md)

- [Learning: a concurrently-open stale branch can silently REVERT a just-merged small PR](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-05-stale-branch-can-silently-revert-a-just-merged-invariant-pr.md)
- [ADR-renumber provenance ('Ordinal note') must use BARE ordinals, not the old ADR-NNN literal](project/learnings/workflow-patterns/2026-07-06-adr-renumber-provenance-note-must-use-bare-ordinals.md)
